    <!-- Verification Logic Script -->
    <script>
        const CHECK_ENDPOINT = 'https://freepanel.in/auth/check-telegram';
        let channelJoined = false;

        function checkInitialVerification() {
            const verified = localStorage.getItem('telegram_verified');
            const verifiedTime = localStorage.getItem('telegram_verified_time');
            if (verified === 'true' && verifiedTime) {
                // Check if verification is still valid (24 hours)
                if ((Date.now() - parseInt(verifiedTime)) < 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000) {
                    document.getElementById('telegram_verified').value = '1';
                    return true;
                }
            }
            return false;
        }

        window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
            const verificationEnabled = false;
            if (!verificationEnabled) {
                document.getElementById('telegram_verified').value = '1';
                return;
            }

            // Restore saved Telegram User ID if available
            const savedUserId = localStorage.getItem('saved_telegram_user_id');
            if (savedUserId) {
                document.getElementById('telegram_user_id').value = savedUserId;
            }

            if (!checkInitialVerification()) {
                document.getElementById('telegramModal').classList.add('active');
                document.getElementById('loginBtn').disabled = true;
                
                // Optional: Auto-verify if ID is saved (uncomment if desired behavior)
                // if (savedUserId) verifyChannelMembership();
            }
        });

        async function verifyChannelMembership() {
            const verifyBtn = document.getElementById('verifyBtn');
            const verifyBtnText = document.getElementById('verifyBtnText');
            const userIdInput = document.getElementById('telegram_user_id');
            const userId = userIdInput.value.trim();
            
            if (!userId) {
                alert('Please enter your Telegram User ID!');
                userIdInput.focus();
                return;
            }

            verifyBtn.disabled = true;
            verifyBtnText.innerHTML = '<div class="spinner"></div>';
            
            try {
                const response = await fetch(CHECK_ENDPOINT, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify({ user_id: userId })
                });
                const data = await response.json();
                
                if (data.success && data.is_member) {
                    // Save verification state
                    localStorage.setItem('telegram_verified', 'true');
                    localStorage.setItem('telegram_verified_time', Date.now().toString());
                    
                    // SAVE USER ID FOR FUTURE USE
                    localStorage.setItem('saved_telegram_user_id', userId);

                    document.getElementById('telegram_verified').value = '1';
                    document.getElementById('telegramModal').classList.remove('active');
                    document.getElementById('loginBtn').disabled = false;
                    verifyBtnText.innerText = 'Verify & Unlock Login';
                } else {
                    alert(data.message || 'Please join the channel first!');
                    verifyBtnText.innerText = 'Verify & Unlock Login';
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Error:', error);
                alert('Verification failed. Try again.');
                verifyBtnText.innerText = 'Verify & Unlock Login';
            }
            verifyBtn.disabled = false;
        }

        // Periodic check
        setInterval(async function() {
            const verified = localStorage.getItem('telegram_verified');
            if (verified === 'true') {
                try {
                    // use saved ID for recheck if possible to avoid issues
                    const savedUserId = localStorage.getItem('saved_telegram_user_id'); 
                    const userIdToCheck = savedUserId || 'recheck_membership';

                    const response = await fetch(CHECK_ENDPOINT, {
                        method: 'POST',
                        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                        body: JSON.stringify({ user_id: userIdToCheck })
                    });
                    const data = await response.json();
                    if (!data.success || !data.is_member) {
                        localStorage.removeItem('telegram_verified');
                        document.getElementById('telegram_verified').value = '0';
                        document.getElementById('telegramModal').classList.add('active');
                        document.getElementById('loginBtn').disabled = true;
                    }
                } catch (e) {}
            }
        }, 60000);
    </script>

    <!-- Verification Logic Script -->
    <script>
        const CHECK_ENDPOINT = 'https://freepanel.in/auth/check-telegram';
        let channelJoined = false;

        function checkInitialVerification() {
            const verified = localStorage.getItem('telegram_verified');
            const verifiedTime = localStorage.getItem('telegram_verified_time');
            if (verified === 'true' && verifiedTime) {
                // Check if verification is still valid (24 hours)
                if ((Date.now() - parseInt(verifiedTime)) < 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000) {
                    document.getElementById('telegram_verified').value = '1';
                    return true;
                }
            }
            return false;
        }

        window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
            const verificationEnabled = false;
            if (!verificationEnabled) {
                document.getElementById('telegram_verified').value = '1';
                return;
            }

            // Restore saved Telegram User ID if available
            const savedUserId = localStorage.getItem('saved_telegram_user_id');
            if (savedUserId) {
                document.getElementById('telegram_user_id').value = savedUserId;
            }

            if (!checkInitialVerification()) {
                document.getElementById('telegramModal').classList.add('active');
                document.getElementById('loginBtn').disabled = true;
                
                // Optional: Auto-verify if ID is saved (uncomment if desired behavior)
                // if (savedUserId) verifyChannelMembership();
            }
        });

        async function verifyChannelMembership() {
            const verifyBtn = document.getElementById('verifyBtn');
            const verifyBtnText = document.getElementById('verifyBtnText');
            const userIdInput = document.getElementById('telegram_user_id');
            const userId = userIdInput.value.trim();
            
            if (!userId) {
                alert('Please enter your Telegram User ID!');
                userIdInput.focus();
                return;
            }

            verifyBtn.disabled = true;
            verifyBtnText.innerHTML = '<div class="spinner"></div>';
            
            try {
                const response = await fetch(CHECK_ENDPOINT, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify({ user_id: userId })
                });
                const data = await response.json();
                
                if (data.success && data.is_member) {
                    // Save verification state
                    localStorage.setItem('telegram_verified', 'true');
                    localStorage.setItem('telegram_verified_time', Date.now().toString());
                    
                    // SAVE USER ID FOR FUTURE USE
                    localStorage.setItem('saved_telegram_user_id', userId);

                    document.getElementById('telegram_verified').value = '1';
                    document.getElementById('telegramModal').classList.remove('active');
                    document.getElementById('loginBtn').disabled = false;
                    verifyBtnText.innerText = 'Verify & Unlock Login';
                } else {
                    alert(data.message || 'Please join the channel first!');
                    verifyBtnText.innerText = 'Verify & Unlock Login';
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Error:', error);
                alert('Verification failed. Try again.');
                verifyBtnText.innerText = 'Verify & Unlock Login';
            }
            verifyBtn.disabled = false;
        }

        // Periodic check
        setInterval(async function() {
            const verified = localStorage.getItem('telegram_verified');
            if (verified === 'true') {
                try {
                    // use saved ID for recheck if possible to avoid issues
                    const savedUserId = localStorage.getItem('saved_telegram_user_id'); 
                    const userIdToCheck = savedUserId || 'recheck_membership';

                    const response = await fetch(CHECK_ENDPOINT, {
                        method: 'POST',
                        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                        body: JSON.stringify({ user_id: userIdToCheck })
                    });
                    const data = await response.json();
                    if (!data.success || !data.is_member) {
                        localStorage.removeItem('telegram_verified');
                        document.getElementById('telegram_verified').value = '0';
                        document.getElementById('telegramModal').classList.add('active');
                        document.getElementById('loginBtn').disabled = true;
                    }
                } catch (e) {}
            }
        }, 60000);
    </script>

    <!-- Verification Logic Script -->
    <script>
        const CHECK_ENDPOINT = 'https://freepanel.in/auth/check-telegram';
        let channelJoined = false;

        function checkInitialVerification() {
            const verified = localStorage.getItem('telegram_verified');
            const verifiedTime = localStorage.getItem('telegram_verified_time');
            if (verified === 'true' && verifiedTime) {
                // Check if verification is still valid (24 hours)
                if ((Date.now() - parseInt(verifiedTime)) < 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000) {
                    document.getElementById('telegram_verified').value = '1';
                    return true;
                }
            }
            return false;
        }

        window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
            const verificationEnabled = false;
            if (!verificationEnabled) {
                document.getElementById('telegram_verified').value = '1';
                return;
            }

            // Restore saved Telegram User ID if available
            const savedUserId = localStorage.getItem('saved_telegram_user_id');
            if (savedUserId) {
                document.getElementById('telegram_user_id').value = savedUserId;
            }

            if (!checkInitialVerification()) {
                document.getElementById('telegramModal').classList.add('active');
                document.getElementById('loginBtn').disabled = true;
                
                // Optional: Auto-verify if ID is saved (uncomment if desired behavior)
                // if (savedUserId) verifyChannelMembership();
            }
        });

        async function verifyChannelMembership() {
            const verifyBtn = document.getElementById('verifyBtn');
            const verifyBtnText = document.getElementById('verifyBtnText');
            const userIdInput = document.getElementById('telegram_user_id');
            const userId = userIdInput.value.trim();
            
            if (!userId) {
                alert('Please enter your Telegram User ID!');
                userIdInput.focus();
                return;
            }

            verifyBtn.disabled = true;
            verifyBtnText.innerHTML = '<div class="spinner"></div>';
            
            try {
                const response = await fetch(CHECK_ENDPOINT, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify({ user_id: userId })
                });
                const data = await response.json();
                
                if (data.success && data.is_member) {
                    // Save verification state
                    localStorage.setItem('telegram_verified', 'true');
                    localStorage.setItem('telegram_verified_time', Date.now().toString());
                    
                    // SAVE USER ID FOR FUTURE USE
                    localStorage.setItem('saved_telegram_user_id', userId);

                    document.getElementById('telegram_verified').value = '1';
                    document.getElementById('telegramModal').classList.remove('active');
                    document.getElementById('loginBtn').disabled = false;
                    verifyBtnText.innerText = 'Verify & Unlock Login';
                } else {
                    alert(data.message || 'Please join the channel first!');
                    verifyBtnText.innerText = 'Verify & Unlock Login';
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Error:', error);
                alert('Verification failed. Try again.');
                verifyBtnText.innerText = 'Verify & Unlock Login';
            }
            verifyBtn.disabled = false;
        }

        // Periodic check
        setInterval(async function() {
            const verified = localStorage.getItem('telegram_verified');
            if (verified === 'true') {
                try {
                    // use saved ID for recheck if possible to avoid issues
                    const savedUserId = localStorage.getItem('saved_telegram_user_id'); 
                    const userIdToCheck = savedUserId || 'recheck_membership';

                    const response = await fetch(CHECK_ENDPOINT, {
                        method: 'POST',
                        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                        body: JSON.stringify({ user_id: userIdToCheck })
                    });
                    const data = await response.json();
                    if (!data.success || !data.is_member) {
                        localStorage.removeItem('telegram_verified');
                        document.getElementById('telegram_verified').value = '0';
                        document.getElementById('telegramModal').classList.add('active');
                        document.getElementById('loginBtn').disabled = true;
                    }
                } catch (e) {}
            }
        }, 60000);
    </script>

    <script>
        function togglePassword(inputId, icon) {
            const input = document.getElementById(inputId);
            if (input.type === 'password') {
                input.type = 'text';
                icon.classList.remove('bi-eye-slash-fill');
                icon.classList.add('bi-eye-fill');
            } else {
                input.type = 'password';
                icon.classList.remove('bi-eye-fill');
                icon.classList.add('bi-eye-slash-fill');
            }
        }
    </script>

    <!-- Verification Logic Script -->
    <script>
        const CHECK_ENDPOINT = 'https://freepanel.in/auth/check-telegram';
        let channelJoined = false;

        function checkInitialVerification() {
            const verified = localStorage.getItem('telegram_verified');
            const verifiedTime = localStorage.getItem('telegram_verified_time');
            if (verified === 'true' && verifiedTime) {
                // Check if verification is still valid (24 hours)
                if ((Date.now() - parseInt(verifiedTime)) < 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000) {
                    document.getElementById('telegram_verified').value = '1';
                    return true;
                }
            }
            return false;
        }

        window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
            const verificationEnabled = false;
            if (!verificationEnabled) {
                document.getElementById('telegram_verified').value = '1';
                return;
            }

            // Restore saved Telegram User ID if available
            const savedUserId = localStorage.getItem('saved_telegram_user_id');
            if (savedUserId) {
                document.getElementById('telegram_user_id').value = savedUserId;
            }

            if (!checkInitialVerification()) {
                document.getElementById('telegramModal').classList.add('active');
                document.getElementById('loginBtn').disabled = true;
                
                // Optional: Auto-verify if ID is saved (uncomment if desired behavior)
                // if (savedUserId) verifyChannelMembership();
            }
        });

        async function verifyChannelMembership() {
            const verifyBtn = document.getElementById('verifyBtn');
            const verifyBtnText = document.getElementById('verifyBtnText');
            const userIdInput = document.getElementById('telegram_user_id');
            const userId = userIdInput.value.trim();
            
            if (!userId) {
                alert('Please enter your Telegram User ID!');
                userIdInput.focus();
                return;
            }

            verifyBtn.disabled = true;
            verifyBtnText.innerHTML = '<div class="spinner"></div>';
            
            try {
                const response = await fetch(CHECK_ENDPOINT, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify({ user_id: userId })
                });
                const data = await response.json();
                
                if (data.success && data.is_member) {
                    // Save verification state
                    localStorage.setItem('telegram_verified', 'true');
                    localStorage.setItem('telegram_verified_time', Date.now().toString());
                    
                    // SAVE USER ID FOR FUTURE USE
                    localStorage.setItem('saved_telegram_user_id', userId);

                    document.getElementById('telegram_verified').value = '1';
                    document.getElementById('telegramModal').classList.remove('active');
                    document.getElementById('loginBtn').disabled = false;
                    verifyBtnText.innerText = 'Verify & Unlock Login';
                } else {
                    alert(data.message || 'Please join the channel first!');
                    verifyBtnText.innerText = 'Verify & Unlock Login';
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Error:', error);
                alert('Verification failed. Try again.');
                verifyBtnText.innerText = 'Verify & Unlock Login';
            }
            verifyBtn.disabled = false;
        }

        // Periodic check
        setInterval(async function() {
            const verified = localStorage.getItem('telegram_verified');
            if (verified === 'true') {
                try {
                    // use saved ID for recheck if possible to avoid issues
                    const savedUserId = localStorage.getItem('saved_telegram_user_id'); 
                    const userIdToCheck = savedUserId || 'recheck_membership';

                    const response = await fetch(CHECK_ENDPOINT, {
                        method: 'POST',
                        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                        body: JSON.stringify({ user_id: userIdToCheck })
                    });
                    const data = await response.json();
                    if (!data.success || !data.is_member) {
                        localStorage.removeItem('telegram_verified');
                        document.getElementById('telegram_verified').value = '0';
                        document.getElementById('telegramModal').classList.add('active');
                        document.getElementById('loginBtn').disabled = true;
                    }
                } catch (e) {}
            }
        }, 60000);

  <script>
  function showLoadingOverlay() {
      document.getElementById('loadingOverlay').style.display = 'flex';
    }

    function hideLoadingOverlay() {
      document.getElementById('loadingOverlay').style.display = 'none';
    }
  function getkey(type){
    showLoadingOverlay();
      var xhr = new XMLHttpRequest();
      //alert("https://yuki.bet/GETKEY/yuri08&type="+type);
      xhr.open("GET", "https://yuki.bet/GETKEY/yuri08&type="+type, true);
      xhr.onload = function () {
        hideLoadingOverlay();
        if (xhr.status >= 200 && xhr.status < 300) {
          var url = xhr.responseText.trim();
          // Strip any PHP warning/notice HTML output before the URL
          var urlMatch = url.match(/(https?:\/\/\S+)$/);
          if (urlMatch) {
            window.location.href = urlMatch[1];
          } else {
            window.location.href = url;
          }
        } else {
          alert('Request failed with status ' + xhr.status);
        }
      };
      xhr.onerror = function () {
        hideLoadingOverlay();
        alert('Request failed');
      };
      xhr.send();
  }
  </script>

    <script>
        // ============ PARTÍCULAS ANIMADAS ============
        const canvas = document.getElementById('particles-canvas');
        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
        canvas.width = window.innerWidth;
        canvas.height = window.innerHeight;

        class Particle {
            constructor() {
                this.x = Math.random() * canvas.width;
                this.y = Math.random() * canvas.height;
                this.size = Math.random() * 2 + 0.5;
                this.speedX = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5;
                this.speedY = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5;
                this.opacity = Math.random() * 0.5 + 0.2;
                this.color = Math.random() > 0.5 ? '#3b82f6' : '#06b6d4';
            }

            update() {
                this.x += this.speedX;
                this.y += this.speedY;
                if (this.x < 0) this.x = canvas.width;
                if (this.x > canvas.width) this.x = 0;
                if (this.y < 0) this.y = canvas.height;
                if (this.y > canvas.height) this.y = 0;
            }

            draw() {
                ctx.fillStyle = this.color;
                ctx.globalAlpha = this.opacity;
                ctx.beginPath();
                ctx.arc(this.x, this.y, this.size, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                ctx.fill();
                ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
            }
        }

        const particles = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < 80; i++) {
            particles.push(new Particle());
        }

        function animateParticles() {
            ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(10, 14, 39, 0.1)';
            ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

            particles.forEach(particle => {
                particle.update();
                particle.draw();
            });

            // Desenhar conexões entre partículas próximas
            for (let i = 0; i < particles.length; i++) {
                for (let j = i + 1; j < particles.length; j++) {
                    const dx = particles[i].x - particles[j].x;
                    const dy = particles[i].y - particles[j].y;
                    const distance = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);

                    if (distance < 150) {
                        ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(59, 130, 246, ${0.2 * (1 - distance / 150)})`;
                        ctx.lineWidth = 1;
                        ctx.beginPath();
                        ctx.moveTo(particles[i].x, particles[i].y);
                        ctx.lineTo(particles[j].x, particles[j].y);
                        ctx.stroke();
                    }
                }
            }

            requestAnimationFrame(animateParticles);
        }

        animateParticles();

        window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
            canvas.width = window.innerWidth;
            canvas.height = window.innerHeight;
        });

        // ============ PALETA DE CORES ============
        const colors = [
            {hex: '#FF0000', name: 'FF0000'}, {hex: '#DC143C', name: 'DC143C'},
            {hex: '#B22222', name: 'B22222'}, {hex: '#8B0000', name: '8B0000'},
            {hex: '#FA8072', name: 'FA8072'}, {hex: '#FF7F50', name: 'FF7F50'},
            {hex: '#FF8C00', name: 'FF8C00'}, {hex: '#FFA500', name: 'FFA500'},
            {hex: '#FFD700', name: 'FFD700'}, {hex: '#F0E68C', name: 'F0E68C'},
            {hex: '#98FB98', name: '98FB98'}, {hex: '#00FF00', name: '00FF00'},
            {hex: '#32CD32', name: '32CD32'}, {hex: '#00FF7F', name: '00FF7F'},
            {hex: '#008000', name: '008000'}, {hex: '#2E8B57', name: '2E8B57'},
            {hex: '#556B2F', name: '556B2F'}, {hex: '#808000', name: '808000'},
            {hex: '#40E0D0', name: '40E0D0'}, {hex: '#00FFFF', name: '00FFFF'},
            {hex: '#00BFFF', name: '00BFFF'}, {hex: '#1E90FF', name: '1E90FF'},
            {hex: '#0000FF', name: '0000FF'}, {hex: '#0000CD', name: '0000CD'},
            {hex: '#00008B', name: '00008B'}, {hex: '#191970', name: '191970'},
            {hex: '#4682B4', name: '4682B4'}, {hex: '#9370DB', name: '9370DB'},
            {hex: '#8A2BE2', name: '8A2BE2'}, {hex: '#9932CC', name: '9932CC'},
            {hex: '#DDA0DD', name: 'DDA0DD'}, {hex: '#EE82EE', name: 'EE82EE'},
            {hex: '#FFB6C1', name: 'FFB6C1'}, {hex: '#FFC0CB', name: 'FFC0CB'},
            {hex: '#FFFFFF', name: 'FFFFFF'}, {hex: '#C0C0C0', name: 'C0C0C0'},
            {hex: '#808080', name: '808080'}, {hex: '#000000', name: '000000'}
        ];

        let selectedColor = null;
        let selectedColorName = null;
        let selectedFormats = new Set();
        let selectedRegion = 'BR';

        function initColors() {
            const grid = document.getElementById('color-grid');
            colors.forEach((color, index) => {
                const btn = document.createElement('div');
                btn.className = 'color-btn';
                btn.style.backgroundColor = color.hex;
                btn.dataset.name = color.name;
                btn.onclick = () => selectColor(color, btn);
                grid.appendChild(btn);
            });
        }

        function selectColor(color, btn) {
            document.querySelectorAll('.color-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('selected'));
            if (selectedColorName === color.name) {
                selectedColor = null;
                selectedColorName = null;
            } else {
                selectedColor = color.hex;
                selectedColorName = color.name;
                btn.classList.add('selected');
                insertAtCursor(`[${color.name}]`);
            }
            updatePreview();
        }

        function insertAtCursor(text) {
            const textarea = document.getElementById('bio-input');
            const start = textarea.selectionStart;
            const end = textarea.selectionEnd;
            const before = textarea.value.substring(0, start);
            const after = textarea.value.substring(end);
            textarea.value = before + text + after;
            textarea.selectionStart = textarea.selectionEnd = start + text.length;
            textarea.focus();
            textarea.dispatchEvent(new Event('input'));
        }

        // ============ BIO INPUT ============
        const bioInput = document.getElementById('bio-input');
        const charCounter = document.getElementById('char-counter');
        const previewBox = document.getElementById('preview-box');

        bioInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
            const len = bioInput.value.length;
            charCounter.textContent = `${len} / 250`;
            charCounter.classList.remove('warning', 'danger');
            if (len > 220) charCounter.classList.add('danger');
            else if (len > 180) charCounter.classList.add('warning');
            updatePreview();
        });

        // ============ FORMATAÇÃO ============
        function toggleFormat(format) {
            const btn = document.getElementById(`btn-${format}`);
            if (selectedFormats.has(format)) {
                selectedFormats.delete(format);
                btn.classList.remove('active');
            } else {
                selectedFormats.add(format);
                btn.classList.add('active');
                const tags = {bold: '[b]', italic: '[i]', curve: '[c]', underline: '[u]', strike: '[s]'};
                insertAtCursor(tags[format]);
            }
            updatePreview();
        }

        // ============ VISUALIZAÇÃO ============
        function updatePreview() {
            let text = bioInput.value || 'Sua bio aparecerá aqui';

            if (!bioInput.value) {
                previewBox.classList.add('empty');
                previewBox.innerHTML = text;
                return;
            }

            previewBox.classList.remove('empty');

            text = text.replace(/\[([0-9A-Fa-f]{6})\]/g, '<span style="color: #$1;">');
            text = text.replace(/\[b\]/g, '<b>');
            text = text.replace(/\[\/b\]/g, '</b>');
            text = text.replace(/\[i\]/g, '<i>');
            text = text.replace(/\[\/i\]/g, '</i>');
            text = text.replace(/\[u\]/g, '<u>');
            text = text.replace(/\[\/u\]/g, '</u>');
            text = text.replace(/\[s\]/g, '<s>');
            text = text.replace(/\[\/s\]/g, '</s>');
            text = text.replace(/\[c\]/g, '<span style="font-style: italic; transform: skewX(-10deg); display: inline-block;">');
            text = text.replace(/\[\/c\]/g, '</span>');

            previewBox.innerHTML = text;
        }

        // ============ MÉTODO DE AUTENTICAÇÃO ============
        function onAuthChange() {
            const method = document.getElementById('auth-method').value;
            const tokenInput = document.getElementById('token-input');
            const passInput = document.getElementById('password-input');

            if (method === 'uid_pass') {
                tokenInput.placeholder = 'Digite o UID';
                passInput.style.display = 'block';
            } else if (method === 'jwt') {
                tokenInput.placeholder = 'Digite o Token JWT';
                passInput.style.display = 'none';
            } else if (method === 'access') {
                tokenInput.placeholder = 'Digite o Token de Acesso';
                passInput.style.display = 'none';
            } else if (method === 'eat') {
                tokenInput.placeholder = 'Digite o Token EAT';
                passInput.style.display = 'none';
            }
        }

        // ============ MODAL DE REGIÃO ============
        function openRegionModal() {
            document.getElementById('region-modal').classList.add('show');
        }

        function closeRegionModal(e) {
            if (e.target === document.getElementById('region-modal')) {
                document.getElementById('region-modal').classList.remove('show');
            }
        }

        function selectRegion(code, text) {
            selectedRegion = code;
            document.getElementById('region-text').textContent = text;
            document.querySelectorAll('.radio-circle').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('selected'));
            document.querySelector(`.radio-circle[data-region="${code}"]`).classList.add('selected');
            document.getElementById('region-modal').classList.remove('show');
        }

        // ============ ATUALIZAR BIO ============
        async function updateBio() {
            const bio = bioInput.value.trim();
            const method = document.getElementById('auth-method').value;
            const token = document.getElementById('token-input').value.trim();
            const password = document.getElementById('password-input').value.trim();

            if (!bio) { showToast('Por favor, digite uma bio!', 'error'); return; }
            if (!token) { showToast('Por favor, digite o token/UID!', 'error'); return; }
            if (method === 'uid_pass' && !password) { showToast('Por favor, digite a senha!', 'error'); return; }

            document.getElementById('loading').classList.add('show');

            try {
                let url = 'https://ob54-asd-long-bio.vercel.app/bio?';

                if (method === 'uid_pass') {
                    url += `bio=${encodeURIComponent(bio)}&uid=${token}&pass=${password}`;
                } else if (method === 'jwt') {
                    url += `bio=${encodeURIComponent(bio)}&jwt=${token}`;
                } else if (method === 'access') {
                    url += `bio=${encodeURIComponent(bio)}&access=${token}`;
                } else if (method === 'eat') {
                    url += `bio=${encodeURIComponent(bio)}&eat=${token}`;
                }

                const response = await fetch(url);
                const data = await response.json();

                document.getElementById('loading').classList.remove('show');

                if (data.error) {
                    showToast(`Erro: ${data.error}`, 'error');
                } else {
                    showToast('Bio Atualizada com Sucesso! ✅', 'success');
                }
            } catch (err) {
                document.getElementById('loading').classList.remove('show');
                showToast(`Falha: ${err.message}`, 'error');
            }
        }

        // ============ TOAST ============
        function showToast(message, type) {
            const toast = document.getElementById('toast');
            toast.textContent = message;
            toast.className = `toast ${type}`;
            setTimeout(() => toast.classList.add('show'), 10);
            setTimeout(() => toast.classList.remove('show'), 3500);
        }

        // ============ INICIALIZAÇÃO ============
        initColors();
        onAuthChange();
    </script>
</body>
</html>

    <script>
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            const encodeBtn = document.getElementById('encodeBtn');
            const decodeBtn = document.getElementById('decodeBtn');
            const copyBtn = document.getElementById('copyBtn');
            const rawInput = document.getElementById('rawInput');
            const encodedOutput = document.getElementById('encodedOutput');
            const toast = document.getElementById('toast');

            // Function to safely base64 encode unicode strings
            function utf8_to_b64(str) {
                const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(str);
                let binary = '';
                for (let i = 0; i < bytes.byteLength; i++) {
                    binary += String.fromCharCode(bytes[i]);
                }
                return window.btoa(binary);
            }

            // Function to safely base64 decode unicode strings
            function b64_to_utf8(str) {
                const binaryString = window.atob(str);
                const bytes = new Uint8Array(binaryString.length);
                for (let i = 0; i < binaryString.length; i++) {
                    bytes[i] = binaryString.charCodeAt(i);
                }
                return new TextDecoder().decode(bytes);
            }

            function rot13(str) {
                return str.replace(/[a-zA-Z]/g, function (c) {
                    return String.fromCharCode((c <= "Z" ? 90 : 122) >= (c = c.charCodeAt(0) + 13) ? c : c - 26);
                });
            }

            function reverseStr(str) {
                return str.split('').reverse().join('');
            }

            // Handle Encode
            encodeBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                const rawCode = rawInput.value.trim();

                if (!rawCode) {
                    showToast('Please enter some PHP code first!', '#ef4444');
                    return;
                }

                try {
                    // Encode the raw text to base64
                    const b64 = utf8_to_b64(rawCode);

                    // Add proprietary lock (rot13 + reverse)
                    const locked_b64 = reverseStr(rot13(b64));

                    // Dynamically construct PHP signature to bypass server WAFs (ModSecurity/Cloudflare)
                    const p1 = "<" + "?php";
                    const p2 = "ev" + "al";
                    const p3 = "base64" + "_decode";
                    
                    // Wrap in the specific eval logic
                    const formattedOutput = `${p1} /* Encoded by Aalyanhost */ ${p2}("?>".${p3}(str_rot13(strrev("${locked_b64}")))); ?>`;

                    // Set output
                    encodedOutput.value = formattedOutput;

                    showToast('Code successfully encoded!', '#10b981');
                } catch (e) {
                    showToast('An error occurred during encoding.', '#ef4444');
                    console.error(e);
                }
            });

            // Handle Decode
            decodeBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                const rawCode = rawInput.value.trim();

                if (!rawCode) {
                    showToast('Please enter encoded PHP code first!', '#ef4444');
                    return;
                }

                try {
                    // Check if it's wrapped in the proprietary pattern
                    const matchSecure = rawCode.match(/strrev\("([^"]+)"\)/);
                    const matchOld = rawCode.match(/base64_decode\("([^"]+)"\)/);

                    let b64 = '';
                    if (matchSecure) {
                        // Unlock the proprietary format
                        b64 = rot13(reverseStr(matchSecure[1]));
                    } else if (matchOld) {
                        b64 = matchOld[1];
                    } else {
                        b64 = rawCode;
                    }

                    // Decode the base64 text
                    let decoded = b64_to_utf8(b64);

                    // Remove existing Aalyanhost comments if present to prevent stacking
                    decoded = decoded.replace(/\/\* (Encoded|Decoded) by Aalyanhost \*\/\n?/g, '');

                    // Set output
                    encodedOutput.value = `/* Decoded by Aalyanhost */\n` + decoded;

                    showToast('Code successfully decoded!', '#10b981');
                } catch (e) {
                    showToast('Invalid base64 or encoding format.', '#ef4444');
                    console.error(e);
                }
            });

            // Handle Copy
            copyBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
                const code = encodedOutput.value.trim();
                if (!code) {
                    showToast('Nothing to copy!', '#ef4444');
                    return;
                }

                try {
                    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(code);
                    showToast('Copied to clipboard!', '#10b981');
                } catch (err) {
                    // Fallback for older browsers
                    encodedOutput.select();
                    document.execCommand('copy');
                    showToast('Copied to clipboard!', '#10b981');
                }
            });

            // Toast Notification logic
            let toastTimeout;
            function showToast(message, color) {
                toast.textContent = message;
                toast.style.background = color;
                toast.classList.add('show');

                clearTimeout(toastTimeout);
                toastTimeout = setTimeout(() => {
                    toast.classList.remove('show');
                }, 3000);
            }
        });
    </script>
